\goodbreak\line{\hskip-4.6cm\hbox to 14.7cm{\hreg\hfill PROPRIUM DE SANCTIS\hfill}\hss}\nobreak\vskip.4cm%
\pdfextension dest name {SCT0} fit
\vskip0.2cm
\vskip.4cm\vbox{\leftskip-3.65cm\noindent\ita%
In memoriis Sanctorum antiphonæ ad\/ {\reg Benedictus} et\/ {\reg Magnificat,} si sint propriæ, sumi debent; 
secus dicuntur aut de Communi, aut de feria occurrente. Antiphonæ quæ infra primis 
tantum verbis suis indicantur, e Communi selectæ, meram vim suggestionis habent.
}\vskip.4cm%
\goodbreak\line{\hskip-4.6cm\hbox to 14.7cm{\bmed\hfill IANUARIUS\hfill}\hss}\nobreak\vskip.6cm%
\pdfextension dest name {SCT1} fit

%Beata Agnes
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Beata Agnes, in medio flammarum, expansis manibus orabat: Te deprecor, venerande, colende, Pater metuende, quia per sanctum Filium tuum minas evasi sacrilegi tyranni, et hostis tentamina immaculato calle transivi; et ecce venio ad te, quem amavi, quem quæsivi, quem semper optavi.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

%Angelus Domini apparuit
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Angelus Domini apparuit in somnis Ioseph dicens: Ioseph, fili David, noli timere accipere Mariam coniugem tuam: quod enim in ea natum est, de Spiritu Sancto est. (TP; alleluia.)\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\gabcsnippet{(c4)Nar(f)ra(f)vé(f)runt(f) lau(f)des(f) Dó(g)mi(f)ni(f) (,) et(f) vir(f)tú(f)tes(e) e(g)ius,(h) (;) Al(h)le(hg)lú(g>)ia(f) al(d)le(fg)lú(g>)ia.(f)  <v>\char401</v>(::) Et(d) mi(f)ra(f)bí(f)li(f)a(f) e(g)ius,(f) quæ(e) fe(g)cit.(h)  <v>\char401</v>(::) Gló(f)ri(f)a(e) Pa(g)tri(h) et(h) Fí(ixhi)li(h)o(h) (,) et(h) Spi(h)rí(h)tu(g)i(h) San(gf~)cto.(f) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Euntes in mundum universum
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Euntes in mundum universum, prædicate evangelium omni creaturæ, alleluia. Qui crediderit et baptizatus fuerit, salvus erit, alleluia; qui vero non crediderit, condemnabitur, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Mc 16,15-16; }{CAO 2739; OP 511}{M}{}{A[25/4,L,B]}%
\greannotation{\med 1f}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)E(d)ún(d)tes(d) in(f>) mun(d)dum(dc) u(f)ni(g)vér(fh)sum,(h) <v>\greheightstar</v>(,) præ(h)di(j)cá(h)te(g) e(h)van(hg~)gé(h)li(g)um(ghg) (,) om(c)ni(d) cre(f)a(e)tú(f)ræ,(g) al(e)le(fe)lú(d)ia.(d) (:) Qui(c) cre(d)dí(f)de(e)rit(d) (`) et(dc~) bap(f)ti(g)zá(h)tus(g) fú(f)e(gh)rit,(h) (,) sal(h)vus(j) e(h)rit,(g) al(h)le(gf)lú(gh)ia;(h) (;) qui(d) ve(fe)ro(d) non(e) cre(f)dí(g)de(f)rit,(gh) (,) con(c)dem(d)ná(f)bi(e)tur,(fg) al(ed~)le(fe)lú(d)ia.(d) (::) E(h) u(h) o(g) u(f) a(gh) e.(gf) (::)}%

\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{Vesp.}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{H}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg Mentibus lætis} ut ad laudes mat.}}\vskip.2cm%
%Evangelii factus sum
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Evangelii factus sum ego minister, secundum operationem virtutis Dei. (TP: alleluia.)\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Col 1,23.25; }{Propre de Mende; ARII 544}{1}{}{A[25/4,V,1]}%
\greannotation{\med 8c}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)E(j)van(j)gé(jkj)lii(jij) <v>\greheightstar</v>(,) fa(j)ctus(j) sum(ji) e(h)go(ij) mi(i)ní(g)ster,(g) (;) se(g)cún(gh~)dum(g) o(g)pe(f)ra(g)ti(h)ó(fe)nem(d) (,) vir(ef)tú(gh)tis(h) <v>\GreDagger</v>() De(g)i.(g) <i>T.P.</i><v>\ \GreDagger</v>(:) De(g)i,(gvFD) al(f)le(fg)lú(g)ia.(g) (::) E(j) u(j) o(h) u(j) a(k) e.(j) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Misit Dominus manum... et replevit
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Misit Dominus manum suam, et tetigit os meum et replevit illud. (TP: alleluia.)\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Ier 1,9, cf. Ps 80,11; }{CAO 3786; ARII 544}{2}{}{A[25/4,V,2]}%
\greannotation{\med 7c}%
\gabcsnippet{(c3)Mi(g)sit(h) Dó(i)mi(j)nus(i) <v>\greheightstar</v>() ma(ij)num(hf~) su(hho)am,(g) (;) et(g) té(i)ti(i)git(h) os(h) me(iji)um(hg/hih) (,) et(f) re(e)plé(fgwhf)vit(g) <v>\GreDagger</v>() il(e)lud.(e) <i>T.P.</i><v>\ \GreDagger</v>(::) il(e)lud,(ef) al(g>)le(fg)lú(e)ia.(e) (::) E(i) u(i) o(j) u(i) a(h) e.(ih) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Semen est verbum
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Semen est verbum Dei, sator autem Christus; omnis qui audit eum, manebit in æternum. (TP: alleluia.)\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Lc 8,11; cf. Mt 7,24; }{CAO 4862; ARII 545}{3}{}{A[25/4,V,3]}%
\greannotation{\med 3a}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)Se(g)men(hi) est(i) <v>\greheightstar</v>() ver(j>)bum(hg~) De(hiwji)i,(hg) (,) sá(g)tor(i) au(h)tem(g>) Chri(efwg)stus;(g) (;) om(g)nis(i) qui(h) au(j>)dit(hg) e(hiwji)um,(hg) <v>\GreDagger</v>(,) ma(g)né(h)bit(gf) in(g) æ(gf)tér(e)num.(e) <i>T.P.</i><v>\ \GreDagger</v>(::) ma(g)né(h)bit(g) in(f) æ(g)tér(gf)num,(ef) al(g)le(gf)lú(e)ia.(e) (::) E(i) u(i) o(j) u(h) a(j) e.(ih) (::)}%

\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{\char403\ br.}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg\lv\pdfextension startlink attr {/Border [0 0 0]} goto name{A[Ap 25/4 11/6 18/10,2V,R]}Annuntiate,\pdfextension endlink}\nobreak\  de Communi apostolorum. {\reg(ARII 545)}}}\vskip.2cm%
%Hæc autem scripta
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Hæc autem scripta sunt, ut credatis quia Iesus est Christus, Filius Dei, et ut credentes vitam habeatis in nomine ipsius, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

%Ancilla fidelis et
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Ancilla fidelis et sponsa electa intravit in cor et gaudium Domini sui, cuius amore languebat, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\vskip.4cm
%O dignissima Christi
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{O dignissima Christi sponsa! quam lux prophetiæ illustravit, zelus apostolicus inflammavit, laurea virginum coronavit, divini amoris incendium consummavit, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\vskip.2cm\goodbreak\line{\hskip-3.6cm\med Die 1: S. Ioseph opificis\hfill}\nobreak\vskip.4cm%
\pdfextension dest name {SCT64} fit
\vskip.4cm\vbox{\leftskip-3.65cm\noindent\ita%
Ubi celebretur hæc memoria modo magis proprio, ad psalmos Laudum matutinarum et Vesperarum sumi possunt 
antiphonæ diei 19 martii, vel sequentes:
}\vskip.4cm%
%Deus mundi opifex
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Deus, mundi opifex, posuit hominem, ut operaretur et custodiret terram, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{cf. Gen 2,15; }{IO 17}{1}{}{A[1/5,L-V,1]}%
\greannotation{\med 1f}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)De(d)us,(dc) mun(f)di(g) ó(f)pi(gh)fex,(h) <v>\greheightstar</v>(,) pó(h)su(g)it(ef) hó(g)mi(fe)nem,(d) (;) ut(g) o(g)pe(e)ra(g)ré(hg)tur(f) (,) et(f) cu(e)sto(f)dí(g)ret(f) ter(fe)ram,(dc) al(d)le(fe)lú(d)ia.(d) (::) E(h) u(h) o(g) u(f) a(gh) e.(gf) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Christus Dei Filius
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Christus, Dei Filius, manibus suis operari dignatus est, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{}{IO 17}{2}{}{A[1/5,L-V,2]}%
\greannotation{\med 1f}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)Chri(d)stus,(ef) De(g)i(ge) Fí(fg)li(fe)us,(d) <v>\greheightstar</v>(,) má(fg)ni(h)bus(g) su(fg)is(f) (,) o(c)pe(d)rá(fg)ri(f) di(f)gná(g)tus(f) est,(ef) al(g)le(fe)lú(d)ia.(d) (::) E(h) u(h) o(g) u(f) a(gh) e.(gf) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Ioseph opifex sancte
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Ioseph, opifex sancte, opera nostra tuere, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{}{IO 18}{3}{}{A[1/5,L-V,3]}%
\greannotation{\med 2*-4d}%
\gabcsnippet{(c3)Io(hi)seph,(iio) <v>\greheightstar</v>(,) ó(hi)pi(j)fex(ih) san(i)cte,(i) (;) ó(fi)pe(h)ra(g) no(fe)stra(f) (,) tu(e)é(de)re,(gxfg) al(h)le(gxg)lú(f)ia.(f) (::) E(i) u(h) o(i) u(j) a(h) e.(i) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Alleluia Christum qui putari
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Alleluia Christum Dominum qui putari dignatus est fabri filius venite adoremus alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\greannotation{\med 5}%
\gabcsnippet{(c3)Al(d!fh~)le(h)lú(hss/@hf~)ia,(h_i_h_/jvI'HGh_f_) (,) Chri(fg)stum(f) Dó(fg)mi(f)num,(f'_) (,) qui(f) pu(f)tá(f)ri(fe) di(f)gná(fg)tus(f) est(f') (`) fa(h)bri(hg) fí(f)li(fgwh_GFg)us,(g.f.) (;) ve(fgwh)ní(e)te,(fgwh) ad(h)o(g)ré(h)mus,(f.) (,) al(f!gw!h>)le(evdeof)lú(fe/ede)ia.(e.d.) (::) Ve(h)ní(h)te,(hi/hss/fgf) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
\pn{186}%
\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{Off. lect.}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{H}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg\lv\pdfextension startlink user{/Subtype/Link /Border[0 0 0] /A<</Type/Action /S/URI /URI(https://gregobase.selapa.net/chant.php?id=15727)>>}{\reg Te pater Ioseph (LHym 370)}\pdfextension endlink}}}\vskip.2cm%
\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{Laudes}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{H}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg\lv\pdfextension startlink user{/Subtype/Link /Border[0 0 0] /A<</Type/Action /S/URI /URI(https://gregobase.selapa.net/chant.php?id=16105)>>}{\reg Aurora solis nuntia (LHym 371)}\pdfextension endlink}}}\vskip.2cm%
%Constituit eum... all. simpl.
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Constituit eum dominum domus suæ * Alleluia alleluia. ℣ Et principem omnis possessionis suæ.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Ps 104,21; }{AM 874}{\char403\ br.}{}{R[1/5,L,R]}%
\greannotation{\med 6}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)Con(f)stí(f)tu(f)it(f) e(g)um(f) (,) do(f)mi(f)num(f) do(f)mus(e) su(g)æ,(h) <v>\greheightstar</v>(;) Al(h)le(hg)lú(g>)ia(f) al(d)le(fg)lú(g>)ia.(f)  <v>\char401</v>(::) Et(f) prín(g)ci(f)pem(f) (,) om(f)nis(f) pos(f)ses(f)si(f)ó(f)nis(e) su(g)æ.(h)  <v>\char401</v>(::) Gló(f)ri(f)a(e) Pa(g)tri(h) et(h) Fí(ixhi)li(h)o(h) (,) et(h) Spi(h)rí(h)tu(g)i(h) San(gf~)cto.(f) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Artem fabri
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Artem fabri fideliter exercens sanctus Ioseph, laboris mirabile refulget exemplar, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{}{IO 18}{B}{}{A[1/5,L,B]}%
\greannotation{\med 7a}%
\gabcsnippet{(c3)Ar(e)tem(gh) fa(ih/ji)bri(i) <v>\greheightstar</v>(,) fi(i)dé(j)li(i)ter(h) ex(i)ér(ij)cens(hf) san(h)ctus(hi) Io(g)seph,(g) (;) la(g)bó(ij)ris(i) mi(h)rá(i)bi(hg)le(e) re(f)fúl(gh)get(f) ex(f)ém(fe)plar,(d) al(ef)le(f)lú(e)ia.(e) (::) E(i) u(i) o(j) u(i) a(h) e.(gf) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{Vesp.}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{H}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg\lv\pdfextension startlink user{/Subtype/Link /Border[0 0 0] /A<</Type/Action /S/URI /URI(https://gregobase.selapa.net/chant.php?id=17636)>>}{\reg Te Ioseph celebrent (LHym 355)}\pdfextension endlink}}}\vskip.2cm%
%Iustus germinabit... all. simpl.
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Iustus germinabit sicut lilium * Alleluia alleluia. ℣ Et florebit in æternum ante Dominum.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{}{AR 584}{\char403\ br.}{}{R[1/5,V,R]}%
\greannotation{\med 6}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)Iu(f)stus(f) ger(f)mi(f)ná(g)bit(f) (,) si(f)cut(e) lí(g)li(g)um(h)  , *(;) Al(h)le(hg)lú(g>)ia(f) al(d)le(fg)lú(g>)ia.(f)  <v>\char401</v>(::) Et(d) flo(f)ré(f)bit(f) in(f) æ(f)tér(g)num(f) (,) an(f)te(e) Dó(g)mi(g)num.(h)  <v>\char401</v>(::) Gló(f)ri(f)a(e) Pa(g)tri(h) et(h) Fí(ixhi)li(h)o(h) (,) et(h) Spi(h)rí(h)tu(g)i(h) San(gf~)cto.(f) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Christus Dominus fabri
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Christus Dominus fabri filius putari dignatus est, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{}{ARII 553}{M}{}{A[1/5,V,M]}%
\greannotation{\med 7c}%
\gabcsnippet{(c3)Chri(g)stus(h) Dó(i)mi(j)nus(i) <v>\greheightstar</v>() fa(j)bri(i) fí(h)li(h)us(g) (,) pu(f)tá(h)ri(g) di(f)gná(g)tus(f) est,(ed) al(ef)le(f)lú(e)ia.(e) (::) E(i) u(i) o(j) u(i) a(h) e.(ih) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
\vskip.2cm\goodbreak\line{\hskip-3.6cm\med Die 2: S. Athanasii\hfill}\nobreak\vskip.4cm%
\pdfextension dest name {SCT65} fit
\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{B}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg\lv\pdfextension startlink attr {/Border [0 0 0]} goto name{A[Doct,,ad lib 5TP]}In medio Ecclesiæ aperuit\pdfextension endlink}\nobreak\ }}\vskip.2cm%
%Quid vobis videtur
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Quid vobis videtur de Christo? Cuius filius est? Dicunt ei omnes: David. Dicit eis Iesus: Quomodo David in Spiritu vocat eum Dominum dicens: Dixit Dominus Domino meo: Sede a dextris meis, alleluia?\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{}{CAO 4533; AM 609.}{M}{}{A[2/5,V,M]}%
\greannotation{\med 4e}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)Quid(efeeo) vo(cdwe!fo)bis(e) <v>\greheightstar</v>() vi(ef)dé(g)tur(f) de(fe) Chri(de)sto?(ddoc) (;) Cu(d)ius(f) fí(fe)li(de)us(efwgh) est?(ge) (:) Di(dhiv)cunt(h) e(gh)i(gf) om(g)nes:(ghg) (`) Da(efwgh)vid.(ge) (:) Di(e)cit(f) e(g)is(ghg) Ie(ed/fe)sus:(dce) (:) Quó(dhiv)mo(h)do(ghg) Da(ef)vid(g) in(f>) Spí(e)ri(de)tu(e) (,) vo(e)cat(f) e(g)um(ghg>) Dó(ed)mi(f)num(e>) di(dce)cens:(e) (;) Di(e)xit(fvEC) Dó(d)mi(e)nus(d) Dó(fvED)mi(e)no(f) me(gh)o:(g) (,) Se(h)de(g) a(f) dex(gh)tris(gf) me(e)is,(ef) al(g)le(gf)lú(e)ia?(e) (::) E(h) u(g) o(h) u(ih) a(gf) e.(e) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
\eject
\vskip.2cm\goodbreak\line{\hskip-3.6cm\med Die 3: Ss. Philippi et Iacobi\hfill}\nobreak\vskip.4cm%
\pdfextension dest name {SCT66} fit
\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{Off. lect.}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{H}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg\lv\pdfextension startlink user{/Subtype/Link /Border[0 0 0] /A<</Type/Action /S/URI /URI(https://gregobase.selapa.net/chant.php?id=18004)>>}{\reg Philippe summæ honoribus (LHym 372)}\pdfextension endlink}}}\vskip.2cm%
%Domine ostende nobis 2
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Domine, ostende nobis Patrem, et sufficit nobis, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Io 14,8; }{CAO 2365; AM 893}{1}{Laudes}{A[3/5,L,1]}%
\greannotation{\med 7c}%
\gabcsnippet{(c3)Dó(hg)mi(hi)ne,(i) <v>\greheightstar</v>(,) o(i)stén(i)de(g) no(ij)bis(i) Pa(hhi)trem,(h) (;) et(h) súf(hf)fi(hih)cit(g) no(e)bis,(f) al(g>)le(fg)lú(e)ia.(e) (::) E(i) u(i) o(j) u(i) a(h) e.(ih) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Tanto tempore vobiscum sum
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Tanto tempore vobiscum sum, et non cognovistis me? Philippe, qui videt me, videt et Patrem meum, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Io 14,9; }{CAO 5113; AM 893}{2}{}{A[3/5,L,2]}%
\greannotation{\med 3a}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)Tan(g)to(hi) tém(i)po(i)re(ji) <v>\greheightstar</v>() vo(h)bí(ij)scum(ji>) sum,(g) (,) et(i) non(j) co(k)gno(kl)ví(kj)stis(hi~) me?(ij) (;) Phil(kj)íp(hi)pe,(i) (`) qui(i) vi(ji)det(hi~) me,(g) (,) vi(g)det(hi) et(hg~) Pa(h)trem(g) me(fe)um,(d) al(g)le(g)lú(ge~)ia.(e) (::) E(i) u(i) o(j) u(h) a(j) e.(ih) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Non turbetur
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Non turbetur cor vestrum neque formidet; creditis in Deum, et in me credite; in domo Patris mei mansiones multæ sunt, alleluia alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Io 14,27.1.2; }{CAO 3938; AM 892}{3}{}{A[3/5,L,3]}%
\greannotation{\med 6f}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)Non(f) tur(ef~)bé(de)tur(ddoc) <v>\greheightstar</v>() cor(cd) ve(dffg)strum(f) (,) ne(ffoe)que(goh) for(ghg>)mí(fvvggof)det;(f) (:) cré(ixfgwh!io)di(h)tis(h) in(ixhiwji>) De(hhog)um,(ghGF) (,) et(e) in(fg~) me(gvFE) cré(d)di(fe)te;(ddoc) (;) in(d>) do(c)mo(d) Pa(c)tris(df~) me(ffg)i(f) (,) man(f)si(h)ó(g)nes(h) mul(f)tæ(fe) sunt,(fog) (;) al(c)le(d)lú(ffg>)ia(g) al(gh)le(f)lú(f)ia.(h) E(h) u(f) o(gh) u(g) a(f) (::) e.()}%
\vskip.4cm
%Invenit Philippus
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Invenit Philippus Nathanael et dicit ei: Quem scripsit Moyses in lege et prophetæ, invenimus Iesum, filium Ioseph Nazareth, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Io 1,45; }{RM 313}{B}{}{A[3/5,L,B]}%
\greannotation{\med 1f}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)In(d)vé(d)nit(f) Phi(d)líp(dc)pus(f) Na(g)thá(f)na(gh)el(h) <v>\greheightstar</v>(,) et(h) di(g)cit(fe) e(cd)i:(d) (;) Quem(d) scri(f)psit(d) Mó(e)y(f)ses(ghg) in(f) le(ixgig~)ge(h) (,) et(f) pro(g)phé(fe)tæ,(d) (;) in(h)vé(hj)ni(h)mus(h) Ie(h)sum,(hg/hiHGgo) (,) fí(e)li(f)um(g) Io(e)seph(goh) a(f) Ná(f)za(fe)reth,(dc) al(d)le(fe)lú(d)ia.(d) (::) E(h) u(h) o(g) u(f) a(gh) e.(gf) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Philippe qui me videt
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Philippe, qui me videt, videt et Patrem meum, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Io 14,9; }{CAO 4291; ARII 556}{1}{Vesp.}{A[3/5,V,1]}%
\greannotation{\med 7a}%
\gabcsnippet{(c3)Phil(e)íp(g!hiv)pe,(i) <v>\greheightstar</v>(,) qui(ig) me(iji) vi(hhi)det,(h) (,) vi(i)det(h) et(f) Pa(hih)trem(g) me(e)um,(f) al(g>)le(fg)lú(e)ia.(e) (::) E(i) u(i) o(j) u(i) a(h) e.(gf) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
\pn{187}%
%Si cognovissetis me
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Si cognovissetis me, et Patrem meum utique cognovissetis; et amodo cognoscetis eum et vidistis eum, alleluia alleluia alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Io 14,7; }{CAO 4879; ARII 556}{2}{}{A[3/5,V,2]}%
\greannotation{\med 7a}%
\gabcsnippet{(c3)Si(e) co(e)gno(-ehg)vis(h)sé(i)tis(j>) me,(i) <v>\greheightstar</v>(,) et(io) Pa(h)trem(j) me(l)um(k) ú(j)ti(i)que(j) co(i>)gno(g)vis(h)sé(ij)tis;(i) (;) et(i) á(ij)mo(i)do(i) co(h)gno(i)scé(hg)tis(f) e(hho)um(g) (,) et(i) vi(i)dí(i)stis(h) e(hg)um,(f) al(f>)le(e)lú(hg>)ia(f) (,) al(gh<)le(i)lú(hg>)ia(f) al(g>)le(fg)lú(e)ia.(e) (::) E(i) u(i) o(j) u(i) a(h) e.(gf) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Si diligitis
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Si diligitis me, mandata mea servate, alleluia alleluia alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Io 14,15; }{CAO 4886; ARII 556}{3}{}{A[3/5,V,3]}%
\greannotation{\med 3a}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)Si(g) di(g)lí(gj)gi(h)tis(gi~) me,(i) <v>\greheightstar</v>(,) man(ij)dá(k)ta(j) me(ji)a(h) ser(g)vá(h)te,(hi) (;) al(g)le(ge)lú(f>)ia(d) (`) al(ggof~)le(h)lú(g)ia(f) al(g>)le(go)lú(e)ia.(e) (::) E(i) u(i) o(j) u(h) a(j) e.(ih) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Si manseritis
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Si manseritis in me et verba mea in vobis manserint, quodcumque petieritis fiet vobis, alleluia alleluia alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

%Ardens est cor meum
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Ardens est cor meum: desidero videre Dominum meum; quæro, et non invenio ubi posuerunt eum, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

%Mulier quid ploras
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Mulier, quid ploras, quem quæris? Illa autem dixit: Tulerunt Dominum meum, et nescio ubi posuerunt eum, alleluia alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Redemisti nos, Domine Deus, in sanguine tuo, ex omni tribu et lingua et populo et natione; et fecisti nos Deo nostro regnum.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\pn{224}%
\vskip.2cm\goodbreak\line{\hskip-3.6cm\med Die 2: In Commemoratione Omnium Fidelium Defunctorum\hfill}\nobreak\vskip.4cm%
\pdfextension dest name {SCT203} fit
\vskip-0.4cm
\vskip.4cm\vbox{\leftskip-3.65cm\noindent\ita%
Omnia ut in Officio Defunctorum.\hfill\break
Quando dies 2 novembris in dominica occurrit, etsi Missa fit de Commemoratione omnium Fidelium Defunctorum, 
celebratur Officium de dominica; Officium autem Defunctorum omittitur. 
Attamen hac die fieri potest celebratio cum populi participatione Laudum matutinarum et Vesperarum de Defunctis.
}\vskip.4cm%
\vskip.2cm\goodbreak\line{\hskip-3.6cm\med Die 3: S. Martini de Porres\hfill}\nobreak\vskip.4cm%
\pdfextension dest name {SCT204} fit
%Benedictus Dominus qui
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Benedictus Dominus qui visitavit plebem suam, et benedictus Deus qui liberavit eam de tenebris et umbra mortis, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
